# izci third-party license texts and notices

Notice snapshot: 2026-10-02, izci 0.3.2.

This document supplements LICENSE.md. Third-party code retains the licenses stated here. It is bundled with the Options license page for offline access in both the PWA and desktop app. Package versions come from package-lock.json, the Windows-target Cargo metadata using src-tauri/Cargo.lock, and the Go dependency lists for the applicable commands. Entries describe source dependency coverage; tree shaking, build-time code generation, optional features, and target selection can result in fewer components in an individual executable. Alternative licenses remain alternatives; reproducing their texts does not impose all alternatives simultaneously.

This is a notice and source-reference document, not a written source offer or a substitute for corresponding-source and replacement/relinking requirements. No GPL/LGPL permission is granted by izci on behalf of another copyright holder.

## Frontend packages and PWA runtime

Applies to the PWA and desktop frontend, except @tauri-apps/api is desktop-only and Workbox is PWA-only. The Svelte support/compiler dependency closure and declaration packages are included conservatively; they do not all execute in the player. General development tools such as Vite, Firebase CLI, Vitest and esbuild are not shipped as application runtimes.

## Embedded replay and encoder libraries

These native libraries are compiled into shipped WASM assets or into encoder JavaScript packages. Their licenses are separate from the license metadata of their wrappers.

### Emscripten loader support

The retained generated loader and AudioWorklet environment shims originated in
the chiptune3 0.8.4 distribution. The Chiptune wrapper and package dependency
have been removed; the original MIT notice is retained for artifact provenance.

Source: https://registry.npmjs.org/chiptune3/-/chiptune3-0.8.4.tgz

Full license/notice text: L014 (LICENSE).

### libopenmpt 0.8.4

License: BSD-3-Clause

Source: https://github.com/OpenMPT/openmpt/tree/libopenmpt-0.8.4

The direct libopenmpt.wasm artifact uses this version. Its Emscripten loader is runtime.js. Its build features identify MINIZ, MINIMP3, STBVORBIS and PLUGINS; it does not identify mpg123. izci adds a JavaScript bridge exposing interactive-interface function pointers.

Full license/notice texts: L031 (LICENSE).

### miniz (inside libopenmpt)

License: MIT

Source: https://github.com/OpenMPT/openmpt/blob/libopenmpt-0.8.4/include/miniz/LICENSE

Full license/notice texts: L032 (LICENSE).

### minimp3 (inside libopenmpt)

License: CC0-1.0

Source: https://github.com/OpenMPT/openmpt/tree/libopenmpt-0.8.4/include/minimp3

Written by Lion (https://github.com/lieff). This MP3 decoder is distinct from the LAME MP3 export encoder and from mpg123.

Full license/notice texts: L033 (LICENSE).

### HivelyTracker AHX/HVL replayer f393ca7c6416f00bcb574b334a7e8b57dcb19eb2

License: BSD-3-Clause

Source: https://github.com/pete-gordon/hivelytracker/tree/f393ca7c6416f00bcb574b334a7e8b57dcb19eb2

The source revision is pinned by scripts/vendor/ahx/build.ps1 for public/vendor/hively-replay.wasm. izci supplies the wrapper and worklet. The server embeds a separately stored Hively replay binary; the two current WASM files are not byte-identical, so the browser source pin is not asserted as independently verified provenance for the server asset.

Full license/notice texts: L038 (LICENSE).

### LAME MP3 encoder 3.100

License: LGPL-2.0-or-later (GNU Library General Public License)

Source: https://sourceforge.net/projects/lame/files/lame/3.100/lame-3.100.tar.gz ; https://lame.sourceforge.io/ ; https://github.com/Vanilagy/mediabunny/tree/v1.52.0/packages/mp3-encoder

Bundled as WASM inside @mediabunny/mp3-encoder 1.52.0 for Converter and Editor export. The MPL license of the JavaScript extension does not replace the LAME license. Copyrights are held by the LAME Project, Mark Taylor, Mike Cheng, and the other contributors identified in LAME source files. The extension documents an encoder-only build with --disable-decoder and --disable-frontend; the older GPL mpglib decoder is excluded from that recipe. Source and rebuild information appears in LICENSE.md. The LGPL text below is the Library GPL version 2, not LGPL 2.1 or 3.

Full license/notice texts: L039 (COPYING); L040 (LICENSE); L041 (Encoding-library license header).

### libFLAC git-3f1ecff8 (20260304)

License: BSD-3-Clause (Xiph.Org license)

Source: https://github.com/xiph/flac/tree/3f1ecff8 ; https://github.com/Vanilagy/mediabunny/tree/v1.52.0/packages/flac-encoder

Embedded by @mediabunny/flac-encoder 1.52.0. The compiled encoder identifies reference libFLAC git-3f1ecff8 20260304. This is the codec library, whose license differs from the GPL FLAC command-line programs.

Full license/notice texts: L042 (COPYING.Xiph).

### libzci reference media libraries (libogg 1.3.5, libopus 1.5.2, libopusfile 0.12)

License: BSD-3-Clause (Xiph.Org license)

Source: https://downloads.xiph.org/releases/ogg/libogg-1.3.5.tar.gz ; https://downloads.xiph.org/releases/opus/opus-1.5.2.tar.gz ; https://downloads.xiph.org/releases/opus/opusfile-0.12.tar.gz

Pinned by libzci/vendor/dependencies.json and acquired/built by libzci/scripts/acquire-media-deps.sh (GNU/Linux) or acquire-media-deps.ps1 (Windows MSVC) into ignored, target-specific .local/libzci/vendor/ locations; never overwriting the frontend/server assets above. libzci-codecs statically links libopusfile 0.12, libopus 1.5.2 and libogg 1.3.5 for its reference Opus provider; libopusfile applies the header gain and container trims. HTTP/TLS support is disabled. Copyrights are held by the Xiph.Org Foundation and the contributors identified in each source tree.

The R4 browser recipe (`libzci/scripts/build-browser-codecs.py`) builds the same digest-verified libraries using Emscripten 4.0.10 into separate ignored target paths. The emitted reference codec includes Emscripten Asyncify/runtime and applicable musl/LLVM support covered by the runtime notices below. It does not reuse or replace ordinary izci codec assets.

Full license/notice texts: L042 (COPYING.Xiph).

## WASM runtime support

The replay and encoder binaries were produced with Emscripten. The runtime/glue and applicable C/C++ runtime notices are retained here. Exact Emscripten/LLVM revisions are not recorded for every precompiled upstream binary; these entries do not assert a single common toolchain version for all codecs.

### Emscripten runtime/glue

License: MIT OR NCSA

Source: https://github.com/emscripten-core/emscripten

Full license/notice texts: L043 (LICENSE (includes Node.js-derived code notice)).

Notices supplied with the local Rust 1.98.1 toolchain for its standard library, including in-tree copyright exceptions and out-of-tree dependency notices. ## Windows SDK loader and compressed-executable notices

Applies only to desktop distributions using the indicated SDK loader and packer.

### Microsoft WebView2 SDK loader 1.0.3800.47

License: BSD-3-Clause

Source: https://www.nuget.org/packages/Microsoft.Web.WebView2/1.0.3800.47 ; https://github.com/wravery/webview2-rs/tree/edc2caf886175ccaebe86078c9cfe1ae2a187328

WebView2LoaderStatic.lib supplied by webview2-com-sys is linked into the MSVC executable. The SDK version is pinned by that crate source revision. The WebView2 browser runtime itself is installed separately and is not bundled into izci. Microsoft SDK NOTICE is reproduced unchanged, including SDK components not necessarily linked by this application.

Full license/notice texts: L246 (SDK LICENSE.txt); L247 (SDK NOTICE.txt).

### UPX / UCL decompression stub 5.2.1

License: GPL-2.0-or-later WITH the UPX special exception for compressed executables

Source: https://github.com/upx/upx/tree/v5.2.1 ; https://github.com/upx/upx/releases/tag/v5.2.1

Copyright (C) 1996-2026 Markus Franz Xaver Johannes Oberhumer; Copyright (C) 1996-2026 Laszlo Molnar; Copyright (C) 2000-2026 John F. Reiser. The default desktop build uses the official unmodified UPX executable and embeds its decompression stub, including LZMA decompression support, in izci.exe. UPX itself is a build tool and is not copied alongside the player. --no-compress builds omit this stub. The complete exception and its restrictions are reproduced below.

Full license/notice texts: L248 (COPYING); L249 (LICENSE (special exception)).

## Optional desktop signaling Go dependencies

Only builds with builtin-signaling embed the Go signaling child. It uses these modules and the Go runtime. It does not embed the full dedicated host, FFmpeg, or the server media dependency graph.

## Android APK dependency notices

The Kotlin/System WebView APK adds the following runtime dependencies. Versions are from the Android Gradle `debugRuntimeClasspath` resolved on 2026-10-04. These libraries are licensed under Apache-2.0; the full license text is reproduced in L004 below. AndroidX is maintained by the Android Open Source Project; Kotlin and JetBrains annotations are maintained by JetBrains; JSpecify is maintained by its contributors.

| Maven artifact | Version | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| androidx.webkit:webkit | 1.14.0 | https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/support/ |
| androidx.annotation:annotation / annotation-jvm | 1.8.1 | https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/support/ |
| androidx.annotation:annotation-experimental | 1.4.1 | https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/support/ |
| androidx.core:core | 1.1.0 | https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/support/ |
| androidx.lifecycle:lifecycle-runtime / lifecycle-common | 2.0.0 | https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/support/ |
| androidx.arch.core:core-common | 2.0.0 | https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/support/ |
| androidx.versionedparcelable:versionedparcelable | 1.1.0 | https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/support/ |
| androidx.collection:collection | 1.0.0 | https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/support/ |
| org.jetbrains.kotlin:kotlin-stdlib | 2.2.21 | https://github.com/JetBrains/kotlin |
| org.jetbrains:annotations | 13.0 | https://github.com/JetBrains/java-annotations |
| org.jspecify:jspecify | 1.0.0 | https://github.com/jspecify/jspecify |

The Android SDK, Android Gradle plugin, Gradle, Kotlin compiler and JVM test dependencies are build/test tools and are not bundled runtimes. The system WebView is supplied and updated by Android rather than redistributed by izci.

### L040

- LAME MP3 encoder: LICENSE

````text
Can I use LAME in my commercial program?  

Yes, you can, under the restrictions of the LGPL (see COPYING
in this folder). The easiest way to do this is to:

1. Link to LAME as separate library (libmp3lame.a on unix or 
   lame_enc.dll or libmp3lame.dll on windows)

2. Fully acknowledge that you are using LAME, and give a link
   to our web site, https://lame.sourceforge.io/:w

3. If you make modifications to LAME, you *must* release these
   modifications back to the LAME project, under the LGPL.
````
